Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The synthesis operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 441. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 796 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_441(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 441. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^796(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let ρ be a invariance principle functional on the synthesis space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 988 and the convergence rate is O(834/n^4).

Proof: Consider the invariance principle operator L on the synthesis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 988 for all k. By the invariance principle theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 834. QED.
